yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷai nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmai kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunilai niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmaikaḷai ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vaittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum kaṟkum tollaiyai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tillai, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēvaiyumillai. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmaikaḷai ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉai aṭaiyalām.
- maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nākai - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭaittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulamai mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamaiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam uṭaiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟainta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivait tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittokai, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭaimaiyē eṉṉai ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ tokai curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷai varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭaiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭai	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷai
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

māṇpukaḻ oḷivīci niṟkum
tamiḻ moḻikkum civaneṟikkum aṭikaḷ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka arumpaṇiyāṟṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻ moḻiyaip pōlavē, aṭikaḷ āṅkilattaiyum vaṭamoḻiyaiyum naṉku kaṟṟavarkaḷ. cāntuṇaiyum kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟa cātaṉai paṭaittavarkaḷ aṭikaḷ. ākavē, tām tēṭiya poruḷaiyellām oru periya nūl nilaiyamākat tikaḻumpaṭi ceytu vaittārkaḷ. aṭikaḷiṉ tamiḻ urai naṭai nūlkaḷ tamiḻiṉ taṉittūymai nilaikkuc cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. eṇṇaṟṟa coṉmārikaḷ poḻintu makkaḷukku uṇarcciyai ūṭṭivanta aṭikaḷ maṟaintu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ uḷḷaḷavum maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷiṉ māṇpukaḻum maṅkātu oḷivīci niṟkum.
- ṭākṭar e.ji. maṇavāḷarāmānucam
tuṇaivēntar, aṇṇāmalaip palkalaik kaḻakam.
(pak. 40)

maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
nuḻaivurai
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
muntai aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum takavārnta perumai peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇaiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yuraikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟai moḻikaḷukkum tantai moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻparappāḷarāyp potunilaik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tantai eṉṉum perumai peṟṟa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭai malaiyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpatai nilai nāṭṭiya niṟaimalai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
 ainteḻuttāl orupāṭai eṉṟu aṟaiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭaiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalaimali pēḻaiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
 vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟaimai pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpatai,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
 marapunilai tirital ceyyuṭ killai 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇai ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citaintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumaiyum kuṟumaiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷai ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takainiṉṟa taṇṭamiḻai - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilaikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟaimalai yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talaippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
 nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivai
 paintamiḻ kākkum paṭai
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭaiyum kalait tiṟap paṭaippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭaiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟai āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟai āṟṟalai 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalaic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantaiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattai
paṉimalai mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amaiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amaintavar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimalai  - imayam; nīla āṟu - nail; amaitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷai, nūlaka iyakkat tantai araṅka nātarum, innāḷ caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakaimaippaṭutti vaittamai perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avai,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟaiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭurai 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nākai aṭikaḷai - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattaic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakaiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammaiyār! ivviṇaiyar koṭaiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉai ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷattai vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟaivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvarai payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum nilaiyil, arumait tantaiyār iyaṟkai eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉaiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyait toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmai tamiḻukku illai. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppillai eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟaikaḷaiyum iḷamaiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟaiyaiyum, nākaiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟaiyaiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟaiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟaiyāl vāṭum aṉṉaikkuc cumaiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākaiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamaiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉai māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpataiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyai niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, tokai nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvai, iṟaiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvai mukappil, ematu patiṉaintām āṇṭil muṟaippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷaiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyataiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uraiyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamaip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nākai intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc caiva cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉai maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉaipeyaril maṟuppurai eḻutiṉār. ataṉaikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷaic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉai viraivilē ceṉṉaikku varuvippōm. nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉai vāḻvu amaiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇaivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉai māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇīya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumai kaṇṭu, pērāciriyaraik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uraiyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattaiyum mūtaṟivaiyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanilai ovvāmaiyāl nākaikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉai: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaraic ceṉṉaikku aḻaittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpikai eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉai kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟait talaivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kalaiñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkalai māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullaippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappālai (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci urai eḻutiṉār.
avvurai nūlai vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟaiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvuraip pāṅkiṉaip piṉpaṟṟip paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷai ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟai urai, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakaikaḷai iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉai viḷaittamai kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakaiya uraikaḷaiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻaintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭattai viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉaik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikkai kuṟaiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟaiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyai muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikaimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. vaiyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikaimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭaivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉai kacaṭukaḷai nīkkiyatu? vaiyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammaic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟaic ceyta arumai eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kārikai āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārait taḻuvutal uṇmai tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapurattai vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattaiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattaiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmi kaḷ valiyuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkaikaḷaiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷai oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. innilaiyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkaikaḷaiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉaikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉaiyār ciṉṉammaiyār, tuṇaiviyār cavuntiram ammai yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvaraik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpikai, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. innilaiyilēyē 27.08.1911il caivat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭai (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. muṉṉēyē avarai aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvai
nām mīḷpārvai oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akavaiyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kaivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullaippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvurai vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉai; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉai; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉai - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikaiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭamai eṉpatu, akattē amaintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷai viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyām. av veḷippaṭṭa nilaiyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravillai! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka nilaiyam, potunilaik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amaippu uruvākum nilaiyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avai iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa nilaiyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭait tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭait tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭait tūṇṭal valimai mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyai veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakaittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takaimai ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉai uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilaināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amaintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmai,
 tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟai yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantaip pēṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉaippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟokai, arumaṇik kuvaiyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷaik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumaimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumait tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 varai mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavai. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyavaiyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vaitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉamai, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷai mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. annilaiyil,
ūḻaiyum uppakkam kāṇpar ulaiviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyai vāymai moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷaiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amaintu tāṅkal, iyaṟkai iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟai muṉvaittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icaik kalaikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākarattai veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉai vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉaip pakutikaḷaiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, uraivēntar auvai cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷai vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantaiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷaiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷaiyum muḻumai muḻumaiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - tokai - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷaiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti varai veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷai ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amaikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumai iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇaiyaiyum makkaḷaiyum uṟavaiyum aṉpaiyum naṇpaiyum oruṅkē iṇaittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇaippaiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟaimalaiya māṇpu
maṟaimalaiyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumai! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalaic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭait tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putaiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉaiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭai! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakaimai! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟaiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymai viḷakkamām!
 puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar nilaiyam,	iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturai - 6
625006.

malaikka vaikkum maṟaimalaiyam!
patippurai
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉai vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmai uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷatai varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇarai aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattait tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭai eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭai niṉaivu kūrum vakaiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyai eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭaippukaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimai yūṭṭiṉar.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻai 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭaippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavai, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavai, peyar terintum pārvaikkuk kiṭṭātavai - eṉap palavakaiyāyk kiṭaittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭaippukaḷ! avaṟṟai `maṟaimalaiyam eṉum talaippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭaivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavalaiyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt ticai nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭaipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭaiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputaiyal palar kaikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷārait tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyattai etirppatilum camaṟkirutattai vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyait tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vaittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavaittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻaippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimaiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukaiyaiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kalaiñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitaikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvai avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta virutai civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamaippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malaimalaiyaṭikaḷ).
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyaraik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟaimiku āṅkilam maṟaivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulamai mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻaikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumaiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
 vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
  maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ maṟaiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷillai vaiyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉaip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉai maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkaiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻaippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamillai.
ceṉṉaiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷait tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talaimuṟaikkum varuṅkālat talaimuṟaikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉai (cātaṉai)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷai muḻumaiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avai kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpatai aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalattai otukki vaittu, tamiḻ nalattaik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟaivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalaip piṉṉiṇaippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻaikkum uṇarvai iḷamaiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmai eḻavaittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avarai eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamaiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟaivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavai! avaṟṟil orupakutiyai yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kaikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vai. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvattai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉaivar kaikaḷaiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumai - caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟai aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟai aṉaivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭaikkum paṇiyaic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumaiyaik karutātu uḻaikkum maṉavurattai muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷai iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭaiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
vallamai cērkkum valimaiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ naintiṭil etunammaik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻaik kākkum uṇarvaip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kaikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷai nāṭṭuṭaimaiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻaikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amaippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.

ulakattamiḻk kaḻakam (1968)
tamiḻiyakkam (1972)
tamiḻc cāṉṟōr pēravai (1998)
tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amaitta ittaku amaippukaḷukku mūla vicaiyākat tikaḻum perumaiyaip peṟuvōr maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavai. avai toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai!
aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉaittup paṭaippukaḷum poruḷvaḻip pirikkappaṭṭu, kālavaricaiyil 34 tokuppukaḷākat tokukkap paṭṭu `maṟaimalaiyam eṉa uṅkaḷ kaikaḷil ippōtu tavaḻkiṉṟatu.
pala talaimuṟaikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum aṟivuk karuvūlam itu!
tamiḻar illantōṟum irukkavēṇṭiya aruntamiḻc celvam itu!
aṟivulakattāl `maṟaimalaiyam varavēṟkappaṭum eṉṉum nampikkai emakkuṇṭu. nūṟ curaṅkattil nuḻaiya aḻaikkiṟōm.
taṉakkeṉa vāḻvatu cāvukkoppākum
  tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻvatē vāḻvatākum
eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷai niṉaivuk kūṟukiṟōm.

kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ	:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr	:	uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625							tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi	:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtaiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷait 				tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉaippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷaippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟaiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amaippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimaik kaḻakam eṉṉum amaippaiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattaiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻaittavar. iḷam talaimuṟaikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkaiyai neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amaippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻaittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakattai niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyum, putiya paṭaippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum, 19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkat tilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷait tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumaiyākac cērttu poruḷvaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil orē vīccil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttirai patittu varupavar.
potunilai
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tantai periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā,  moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōrai vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkai kaḷai niṟaivēṟṟa ayarātu uḻaittu varupavar.

mullaippāṭṭu ārāycciyurai
1962il caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam  patippitta
nūliṉai mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
innūlaip paṟṟiya kuṟippurai...
iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟivatilum, kaṇṭavāṟē colvatilum nappūtaṉār koṇṭuḷḷa tiṟaṉ, iṉpam payakkum vakaiyil viḻuppamuṟa viḷaṅkum poruṭkōvai ākiya vaṟṟai aṭikaḷār pārāṭṭukiṟār. poruḷkaḷukku ēṟpa aṭikaḷ metuvākavum viraivākavum iṭaiyiṭaiyē teṟṟuppaṭṭum ceṉṟu, ōcaiyiṉpam māṟi māṟip payakkum vakaiyil amaintuḷḷataip pārāṭṭum aṭikaḷār, ēṉaiya oṉpatu pāṭṭukaḷaip pōla mullaippāṭṭu mika uyarnta tīñcuvai naṭaiyiṉaip peṟātu kuṟaipaṭutalaiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
mullaippāṭṭil poruḷ iṭaiyaṟal paṭukiṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, poruḷ poruntumiṭaṅkaḷ, poruḷ muṟṟiyum muṟṟāmal viritalaṭaikiṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, poruḷ mutircciyuṭaṉ muṭitaluṟumiṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai `mullaippāṭṭil nīḷacceṉṟu poruntum coṟṟoṭar muṭivu eṉṟa talaippil aṭikaḷār tam āyvuttiṟam veḷippaṭum vakaiyil viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻariṉ vaḻakkavoḻukka varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷai ārāyntu nimittam pārttal, kāṭṭil pāṭivīṭu, makaḷirōṭu pāṭi vīṭṭil taṅkal, yāṉaikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil payiṟci peṟal, ayalavarkaḷ aracarkaḷatu paḷḷiṟaik kāvalarāyiruntamai. kamma vēlai ceytal, ēḻaṭukku māḷikaikaḷ, palvakai iṉpa arum paṇṭaṅkaḷ, tamiḻaka maṉṉar nāṟpaṭai vaḷam ākiya uṇmaikaḷait tam ārāyccik kuṟippil amaittuḷḷār.
mullaippāṭṭārāycci mutaṉ mutalil eḻunta centamiḻc ceyyuḷ uṇmaiyārāycci viḷakkamākum
- nā. ceyappirakācu
maṟaimalai aṭikaḷāriṉ  ilakkiya paṭaippukaḷ
pak. 5,6


Preface to the first edition
 “Poetry is simply the most beautiful impressive and widely effective mode of saying things and hence its importance
 – Matthew Arnold.
To a mind brooding on the silent beauty exhibited in the varied phenomena of nature, to an interest diving deep into the mysteries that lie in the inmost coners of life, to a soul soaring high into ethereal regions of religion and philosophy, to a spirit seeking serene rest in moral sanctity and Divine grace, nothing appears so permanently beautiful, so certainly impressive and effective as the study of a fine piece of poetry. Not only does a fine poem delight us by presenting a faithful picture of nature’s charms and beauties but it also impresses us with the immediate presence of a benign principle that manifests itself in all that is bright and beautiful. While it thus kindles in man the thought about a supreme being of unlimited love and kindness, it calls forth also from him love and sympathy for all animate beings which like every one of us pursue certain lines of development and fulfill certain purposes in life each in its way. It is this love, this sympathy that marks him out as superior to lower animal kingdom; it is this special quality that refine and ennobles his entire being.
Further, the soothing power of poetry is great and its influence on our mind is mild and sweet. To hearts eaten away by sorrow and worldly cares the reading of poetry comes as a healing balm; to hearts chilled by the icy hand of cruelty it appears like the warning rays of the morning Sun; and to hearts stained with the blackest crime and the basest vice it comes as the washing water of a crystal rill. Ah:! How lovely are the changes wrought by poetry in the mind of man; and how profound  and permanent are the moral effects it brings into his inward nature:!
To me the study of poetry has been a welling fountain of delight and ever will it continue to be so to the very end of my life on this Earthly plane. For years together I have been devoting my time to a close, careful and diligent study of the ancient classical poems of the Tamil language and have been drinking deep the ineffable sweetness that was there strode in. The pure simplicity of thought, the close and minute observation of nature, the vivid and sublime portraits presented of the social, moral religious and intellectual conditions of the hoary Tamilian life, the energy and artistic beauty beaming through with a sterling freshness and last, but not least the great historic value which it possesses – all combine to invest Tamil Poetry with a peculiar charm and splendour that can hardly be surpassed by the poetry of any great language in the world. These characteristics of Tamil Poetry accord to Tamil quite a unique place in the history of cultivated languages in the civilized world.
But sad to relate that the Tamil poems prescribed as text books for the B.A and F.A examination in the university of Madras/ have attracted little or no attention of the students and that a study of them has even been looked upon as useless and tiresome by some who had been led to revel in stories of religious and mythological character and who had thereby lost all seriousness and all appreciation of natures charms. This has been due partly to the teaching of the Pandits and their bulky volumes of notes which mainly consist in giving word for word meanings and turgid and trivial grammatical notes and partly to some prescribed Tamil texts in prose and poetry which/ for the most part contain silly and crude religious myths translated from Sanscrit Puranas and which by no means represent the true character of original Tamil classics. 
I do not mean, however, to deny the fact that one or two classical poems are occasionally prescribed as text – books by the scrupulous members of the Tamil Board, But I only intent to say that even these brilliant grms get buried deep in the heap of filthy poems forthcoming as text – books by their side. The sparkling glitter of the farmer is swallowed up in the intense darkness of the latter;; and the study of Tamil Poetry therefore appears to our gaze as though it were surrounded by an impenetrable gloom.
In spite of this disadvantage I had had an earnest desire to direct the attention of students to a sound critical study of at least the few prescribed texts of classical poems and see what effect would be produced on them when they are taught in the light of modern poetry – criticism in general. Although I felt incompetent for the task, yet I ventured to make the attempt which as I afterwards found to my great satisfaction, proved highly successful not only among the students of my college but also among those who stand outside the pale of university learning and study Tamil in private out of pure love. The benefits that had accrued to students from a critical and comparative study of poetry became patent and ever since the time that I attempted my critical methods of teaching poetry my scheme was put into practice only orally. But an occasion presented itself to come for bringing my critical methods of teaching into writing also. While I was engaged in teaching Mullaippattu to my students of the senior B.A. class, they requested me to write a critical commentary on this beautiful idyll on the new lines and subscribed in advance to defray the cost of printing. In compliance with their request I undertook the task with pleasure and finished it. I take this opportunity to express my thanks to my students of the senior B.A. class for this and many other acts of courtesy and kindness they had extended to me.
Mullaippattu is an ancient classical Tamil idyll of 103 lines sung in honour of the pandian king Nedunchelian by his court bard Napputhanar. How Nedunchelian, one having gone on a expedition to meet the combined forces of seven powerful kings in the field at Talayalanganam and routed them all in the fierce battle that ensued, returns with feat splendour and magnificence and with trophies of victory to kiss his loveliest wife and how this beautiful maiden that had been left alone in a country villa with a deep sorrow at the separation of her beloved husband, is lying down on her couch her chaste bosom heaving up with a sigh of consolation at his shortly expected return, are all related most vividly and very picturesquely in the poem. The poem is remarkable alike for simplicity of thought, beauty of expression and noble sentiments. The imaginative hold which it has on the mind of the reader is strong and elevating. The interest of the plot is sustained thought out but the poet with great delicacy and subtlety that characterize a master mind. The rhythm and harmony of style vary every now and then and adhere closely to the dignified march of the sense, while the diction, though rugged at times, is one the whole, chaste and elegant replete with genuine elements of Tamil. The Sanscrit words have mingled in it at the rare of two per cent and hence the date of its composition falls about the first century after Christian era.
In commenting on this excellent poem I have followed the main lines of literary criticism inculcated by the able and profound critic professor William minto;; for his critical methods are authentic and help much towards a true and clear under standing of the work taken to be scrutinized. From this critical point of view, I have given a historical account of the age that gave birth to such a splendid poem as Mullaippattu and of the indluence which that age had exerted on this poem. It must be remembered that the true nature of a work of art cannot be comprehended unless the nature and conditions of the times in which it had sprung are distinctly understood. I have also availed myself of the grand views expressed on poetry by such great masters as Milton and Ruskin and have even translated freely into Tamil one or two passage from their writings.
In my analysis of the poem I have adhered closely to the original idea conceived and expressed by the poet, rather than follow the mangled and distorted meanings given by the old commentator Nachinarkkiniar. The old commentator, discarding the simple and natural order and setting in which the subject matter or the idyll is disposed, has torn the excellently woven fabric of the poet to pieces and glues them again together with his own vapid thought is such a fantastic manner s to make the poem seem most unnatural and its structure highly ridiculous. And in elucidating the text I have, therefore, inserted, wherever necessary, short critical notes which would make the inaptitude of the commentators meaning at one intelligible to all reflective minds. Pandit V. Swamintha Aiyar of the Government College at Kumbakonam and pandit C. Tiruchitrambalam Pillai of coimbatore having been treaty misled by the commentary of Nachinarkkiniar, have in their editions, added notes which, without following the sense of the poem, simply mar the flow and natural setting of its poetic thoughts. But of the two I wish to recommend to students Mr. Tiruchitrambalam’s edition, since his comments and critical introduction seem to me more valuable for a clear understanding of the old commentary, though not of the poem that that of Mr. Swaminatha Aiyar. In the course of my criticism I need hardly say that I have differed as widely from the old commentator as from the two Pandits, But I have fully stated my reasons and authority for doing so.
I conclude these few prefatory remarks with a fervent hope that these Tamil students who take a deep interest in the study of this poem, will find in the following critical commentary a better means of getting at a true and appreciative knowledge of this only genuine relic of a great and old Tamil poet Napputhanar and will interest themselves by further devoting a few of their leisure hours to the study of Tamil classics and extend their knowledge of Tamil wider than it is at present.
Madras Christian college 
28th September 1903					Vedachalam
nāṉkām patippiṉ mukavurai
iṟṟaikku irupatteṭṭāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ yām im mullaip pāṭṭārāycci yuraiyai eḻutiya kālattup paḻaiya caṅkat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷākiya akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇai, kuṟuntokai mutalāyiṉa acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttappaṭavillai. ataṉāl yām payiṉṟa tolkāppiya vuraiyiliruntē ivvārāycciyuraikku vēṇṭiya mēṟkōṭ ceyyuṭkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭalāyiṉēm. appōtu am mēṟkōḷ uḷḷa nūlkaḷaik kuṟippiṭutal iyalātiruntatu. maṟṟu, in nāḷilō eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷ avvaḷavum veḷivarum pēṟu kiṭaittiruttaliṉ, yām ippatippiṉkaṇ ovvoru mēṟ  kōḷukkum avai vantuḷḷa nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaik kuṟittirukkiṉṟēm.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, muṟpatippukaḷil illāta cila pakutikaḷum viḷakka vuraik kuṟippukaḷum ippatippiṉkaṭ putiyavāyc cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṉum, innūliṉ urainaṭaic cuvaiyai muṉṉaiyilum mikutip paṭuttal vēṇṭip, palappala coṟṟiruttaṅkaḷum coṟṟoṭart tiruttaṅkaḷum āṅkāṅkuc ceytirukkiṉṟēm.
ivaiyallāmalum, muṟpatippukkaḷil iṭaiyiṭaiyē viraviyirunta ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyuṅ kaḷainteṭuttu, ivvurainaṭaiyaic cālavun tūya taṉit tamiḻākkiyirukkiṉṟēm.
iṅṅaṉamāka urainaṭai vakaiyilum uraik kuṟippu vakaiyiluñ ceyyappaṭṭa cila pala māṟutalkaḷait tavirap poruḷ vakaiyil ētoru māṟutalum itaṉkaṭ ceyyappaṭavillai. ākavē, muṟpatippukkaḷaip payiṉṟavarkaḷ ip patippiṉkaṇ tikaippuṟattakka poruḷ māṟutal ētuñ ceyyappaṭavillai yeṉpatum, innūliṉ tamiḻc cuvaiyiṉai mēlum mikuti ceytaṟku vēṇṭiya aḷavē cila pala cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉpatum aṟiñarkaḷ aṟivārkaḷāka! in nūliṉ muṟpatippiṉaip payiṉṟu makiḻntavarkaḷukku ip patippu am makiḻcciyiṉai mikuti ceyyumeṉṉum nampikkaiyumuṭaiyēm.
pallāvaram potunilaik kaḻakam	iṅṅaṉam
	tiruvaḷḷuvar yāṇṭu, 1961	maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ
mūṉṟām patippiṉ mukavurai
 muruku porunāṟu pāṇiraṇṭu mullai
peruku vaḷamaturaik kāñci - maruviṉiya
kōlaneṭu nalvāṭai kōlkuṟiñci paṭṭiṉap
pālai kaṭāttoṭum pattu
eṉṉum paḻaiya pāṭṭiṉkaṭ kaṇṭa tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai mutal malaipaṭukaṭām īṟāṉa aruntamiḻp pāṭṭukaḷ pattiṉuḷ aintāvatāy niṟpatu mullaippāṭṭākum. taṉ āruyirkkaṇavaṉaip pirintu varuntiyirunta talaiviyiṉ nilaimaiyum, pakaiyaracar mēṟpōrpuriyac ceṉṟa avaḷ koḻunaṉ kārkālat tuvakkattē ap pōrviṉaiyai muṭittut taṉ maṉaivipāṟ ṟirumpivarum vakaimaiyum ic ceyyuḷiṉ kaṇ mikavum aḻakāka eṭuttuc collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭuc ciṟitēṟakkuṟaiya iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. itaṉai iyaṟṟiya āciriyar; kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattup poṉvāṇikaṉār makaṉār nappūtaṉār eṉṟu collappaṭuvar. ip pāṭṭaittavira ivvāciriyarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavāka vēṟu evaiyum ituvaraiyum veḷippaṭa villai.
kaṟpōruṇarvaik kavarun tiṟattatāy, aḻakiya iyaṟkaip poruḷ nilaikaḷai iruntavāṟē eṭuttu moḻivatāy, nūṟṟu mūṉṟaṭikaḷiṟ curuṅkiya pāvāy iruppiṉun tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nalaṅkaḷai viḷakkamākat tēṟṟuvatāy uḷḷa im mullaip pāṭṭu ki.pi. 1903 ām āṇṭu kalainūṟ pulamaikkup payilum māṇākkarkkup pāṭamāka vantatu. ceṉṉaik kiṟittuvak kallūriyil appōtu yām tamiḻāciriyarāy iruntu māṇākkarkkut tamiḻnūl aṟivuṟutti vantamaiyāl, im mullaip pāṭṭiṟkum uraivirittu uraikkalāṉēm. icceyyuṭku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uraiyaiyuṅ kūṭavē viḷakki varukaiyiṟ, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟiya āciriyar koṇṭa poruṇmuṟai oru pakkamākavum, uraikārar koṇṭa poruṇmuṟai maṟṟoru pakkamākavum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇaṅkātāy māṟupaṭṭu niṟṟal kaṇṭu māṇākkar peritum iṭarppaṭuvārāyiṉar. atuvēyumaṉṟi, ākkiyōṉ amaitta coṟṟoṭar neṟiyaic citaittuc coṟkaḷaiyum aṭaimoḻi
kaḷaiyum oru muṟaiyumiṉṟip pirittukkūṭṭip poruḷuraikkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarurai ciṟitum ēlāvuraiyēyāmeṉṟum avar karutuvārāyiṉar. muṉṉarē ākkiyōṉ karuttaiyoṭṭi vēṟōr uraiceytu vaittirunta yām ataṉaiyeṭuttu avarkku viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉēmāka, atu kaṇṭu avarellān tamaturai ākkiyōṉ karutiya poruḷai nērniṉṟu viḷakkuvatoṭu, paṇṭaikkālattut taṇṭamiḻc ciṟappukaḷaip piṉṟaik kālattukkēṟṟapaṭi teṟṟeṉa naṉku terippatāyum iruttaliṉ tamatu virinta iv vārāycciyuraiyiṉaiyē patippiṭṭu emakkut tantaruḷal vēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭu atu patippiṭutaṟkāñ celavum oruṅku cērntu muṉ utaviṉar. aṅṅaṉam utaviya am māṇākkarkku yām eṉṟum naṉṟiceluttak kaṭamaip paṭṭirukkiṉṟēm.
uṭaṉē yām eḻutiya mullaippāṭṭārāycciyurai acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭamaiyiṉ, ataṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷ avvāṇṭiṉ māṇākkarkkum piṟarkkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. avvurai nūlai vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum, ēṉait tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟaiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvuraip pāṅkiṉaip peritum viyantu pārāṭṭi, ammuṟaiyiṉaip piṉpaṟṟip paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷai ārāyvatiṟ kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputu muṟaiyurai tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakaikaḷai iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcciyiṉai viḷaittamai kaṇṭu, iṉi ittakaiya uraikaḷaiyē eḻututal vēṇṭumeṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻaintatu.
ki.pi. 1906 ām āṇṭu kalainūṟ pulamaikkup payilum māṇākkarkkup pattuppāṭṭukkaḷil oṉṟāṉa paṭṭiṉap pālai eṉṉum aruntamiḻc ceyyuḷ pāṭamāy vantatu. yām mullaippāṭṭiṟ keḻutiya ārāycciyurai yiṉaippaṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟa avvāṇṭiṉ māṇākkarkaḷun tamakkup pāṭamāy vanta paṭṭiṉappālaikkum ataṉaip pōlavē or ārāycciyurai eḻutittarumpaṭi vēṇṭi, ataṉaip patippiṭutaṟkāñ celaviṉ poruṭṭut tāmum oruṅkucērntu poruḷutavi ceytaṉar. am māṇākkar ceyta vutavikkum yām perituṅ kaṭamaippaṭṭiruk kiṉṟēm.
tirumpavuṅ ki.pi.1910 ām āṇṭu kalainūṟ pulamaikkup payilum māṇākkarkkum mullaippāṭṭup pāṭamāka vantatu. itaṟkiṭaiyil mutaṟ patippiṭṭa ematu mullaip pāṭṭārāycciyuraip puttakaṅkaḷ ellāñ celavaḻintu pōṉamaiyiṉ, avvāṇṭiṉ māṇākkar kaḷuñ ceyta vēṇṭu kōṭ kiṇaṅki avar tāmum oruṅkucērntu muṉṉart tanta poruḷkoṇṭu aḵtiraṇṭām muṟaiyum patippiṭappaṭṭatu. iraṇṭām patippup puttakaṅkaḷuñ celavāy viṭṭamaiyāl, ippōtitaṉai mīṇṭum patikkalāyiṉēm. ippatippiṉkaṭ pala tiruttaṅkaḷum pala māṟutalkaḷuñ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ematiṭamuḷḷa ōr ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyaiyum accup puttakattaiyumoppiṭṭup pārttu mullaippāṭṭuc ceyyuḷiṉ kaṇṇuñ cila tiruttaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
mullaip pāṭṭup payilvōr piṟanūl utavi peritum vēṇṭāmal eḷitil ataṉaik kaṟṟaṟitaṟ poruṭṭu muṉṉiraṇṭu patippukkaḷilum illāta pala viḷakka uraik kuṟippukaḷ ippōtitaṉkaṭ putiyavāyc cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. atuvēyumaṉṟi, ulaka vaḻakkattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷait tavirttu mullaippāṭṭil vanta ēṉai ellāc coṟkaḷaiyum akara varicaippaṭutti avaṟṟiṟkellām poruḷkaḷ eḻutiyiruk kiṉṟēm. iv varuñcoṟporuḷ varicaiyiṉ utavi koṇṭu icceyyuṭporuḷ uṇarntu koḷvatu evarkkum eḷitēyām. iṉi, ivvaruñ coṟkaṭkup poruḷ varaiyuṅkāl im mullaippāṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya kālattil ataṉkaṇ vanta coṟkaṭku vaḻaṅkiya poruḷkaḷaiyum, akkālattai aṭuttut tōṉṟiya cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷil avaṟṟiṟku vaḻaṅkiya poruḷkaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟēm. oru  ceyyuḷ vaḻaṅkiya kālattum ataṉai yaṭuttuvanta kālattum ac ceyyuṭ coṟkaṭkup poruṭeḷivatu acceyyuḷ ākkiyōṉ karuttai naṉkaṟintu kōṭaṟkuk karuviyāmātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉañ coṟ poru ṭuṇivikkum muṟaiyait tamiḻārāy vōr aṉaivaruṅ kaippaṟṟi oḻukuvarāyiṉ namatarumaic centamiḻmoḻi cālavum viḷakkam uṭaiyatākit tikaḻum.
iṉi, ivvārāycciyuraiyiṉkaṇ maṟṟoru mutaṉmai yāṉa cīrtiruttamuñ ceytirukkiṉṟēm. toṉṟu toṭṭa ciṟappum, ilakkaṇa ilakkiya varampun, taṉakkeṉap paṉṉūṟāyirañ coṟkaḷum vāyntu, iṉṟukāṟum vaḻakku vīḻātu uyirōṭu ulāvip paṉṉūṟāyiram makkaṭkup peritu payaṉpaṭṭu varum namatu iṉiya centamiḻ moḻiyai ayalmoḻic coṟkaḷ viravāmaṟ pātukāttut tūyatāy vaḻaṅki ataṉai vaḷampeṟac ceyvatu tamiḻarāyiṉār ovvoru varkkum iṉṟiyamaiyāta kaṭamaiyām. cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāyt tōṉṟi ilakkaṇa ilakkiya varampillātu tamakkeṉac cila coṟkaḷēyuṭaiya moḻikaḷaiyum, ulaka vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāmal iṟantupaṭṭa moḻikaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṟkuriyārum avaṟṟiṟku uriyārpōṟ ṟammai eṇṇikkoḷvārum avaṟṟait tūyavāy vaḻaṅkavum avaṟṟai uyirppikkavum ōvātu muyaṉṟuvara, ellā nalaṅkaḷum oruṅkuṭaiya namatarumaic centamiḻ moḻiyai nammaṉōr payilātum pātukāvātuṅ kaiviṭṭiruttal nirampavum iraṅkaṟpālatoṉṟām. iṉiyēṉum avar aṅṅaṉam maṭintirāmaip poruṭṭu, nammaṉōriṟ kaṟṟavarāyiruppōr āriyam āṅkilam mutalāṉa piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaik kalavāmaṟ ṟaṉittamiḻiṟ pēcavum eḻutavuṅ kaṭaippiṭiyāyp paḻakivaral vēṇṭum. itaṉai muṉnaṭantu kāṭṭum poruṭṭu, itaṟkumuṉ yām eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṟ pukunta ciṟcila ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷaiyum annūlkaḷait tirumpap patippiṭṭuvarum ippōtu muḻutuṅ kaḷaintu viṭṭu, avai niṉṟa iṭattiṟ ṟūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷaiyē nirappi varukiṉṟēm. im mullaippāṭṭārāycci yuraiyiṉ kaṇṇum muṟpatippukaḷilirunta ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷai nīkki avaṟṟiṟkīṭāṉa centamiḻc coṟkaḷaiyē ippatippiṉkaṭ peytu vaittirukkiṉṟēm. eṉṟāluṅ, kālanilaikkum nākarika vaḷarccikkum icaiyac cila ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷaiyuñ cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum orōviṭaṅkaḷil mikac ciṟuka eṭuttāḷutal vaḻuvaṉ ṟeṉpatūum aṟiñarkku uṭaṉpāṭāmeṉka. it taṉmaiyavāṉa ārāycciyuraikaḷ eḻutuvataṟku nalvaḻi kāṭṭiṉa āṅkilamoḻi nallicaip pulavarkku yām eḻumaiyum naṉṟi pārāṭṭuṅ kaṭamai uṭaiyēm.

1. pāṭṭiṉiyalpu
mullaippāṭṭu eṉpataip paṟṟit teriya vēṇṭuvaṉa ellām ārāyummuṉ, pāṭṭu eṉpatu ettaṉmaiyatu? eṉṟu ārāyntu aṟintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. piṉṟaik kālattut tamiḻppulavar pāṭṭeṉpatu iṉṉateṉṟē aṟiyārāyp putupputu muṟaiyāṟ coṟkaḷaik kōttup poruḷ āḻamiṉṟic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟukiṉṟār. paṇṭaik kālattut taṇṭamiḻp pulavarō pāṭṭu eṉpataṉ iyalpai naṉkaṟintu nalamuṭaiya ceyyuṭkaḷ palap pala iyaṟṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉam muṟkālattārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāṭṭiṉ iyalpoṭu māṟupaṭṭup piṟkālattār uṇmai piṟaḻntu pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷaik kaṇṭu māṇākkar pāṭṭiṉiyalpu aṟiyātu mayaṅkuvarākaliṟ, pāṭṭu eṉpatu iṉṉateṉpataṉai oru ciṟitu viḷakkuvām.
ulakaiyaṟkaiyiṟ kaṇ mutalāṉa pulaṉkaḷukku viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum aḻakaiyellān taṉṉakattē neruṅkap potintuvaittup, piṉ avaṟṟai nam aṟiviṉiṭattē pulappaṭumvaṇṇan tōṟṟuvittup, poruḷ nikaḻcciyoṭu māṟupāṭutal illā iṉiya ōcaiyuṭaṉ icaintu naṭaipeṟum iyalpiṉai uṭaiyatutāṉ pāṭṭeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. iṉṉum eṅkeṅku nam aṟivait tamvayappaṭuttukiṉṟa pēraḻakum pēroḷiyum peruntaṉmaiyum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavō aṅkellām pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum. itaṉai viḷakkik kāṭṭumiṭattup, pēraḻakāṟ ciṟanta or araci tāṉ mēṟpōrttirunta nīlap paṭṭu āṭaiyiṉaic ciṟitu ciṟitāka nīkkip, piṉ ataṉaic curuṭṭik kīḻē eṟintu viṭṭut tuyil oḻintu, oḷiviḷaṅku taṉ naḷimukaṅkāṭṭi eḻuntatai yoppa, iruṭkūṭṭañ curuṇṭu maṭaṅki alaikaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu aṭaṅkiviṭumāṟu iḷaiya ñāyiṟu urukkit tiraṭṭiya pacumpoṟ ṟiraḷaipōlat taḷataḷaveṉat kīḻt ticaiyil tōṉṟavum, atticaiyiṉ parappellām poṉ uraitta kaṟpōṟ polintu tikaḻavum, pacumai poṉmai nīlam civappu veṇmai mutalāṉa niṟavēṟupāṭuḷḷa poṉ veḷḷikaḷ uruki ōṭukiṉṟa nilam pōla vāṉ iṭamellām pala vaṇṇamāy virintu viḷaṅkavum, kariyamukilka ḷellāñ cevvarakku vaḻitta akaṉṟa tiraiccīlaikaḷ pōlavum veḷiyē mukilkaḷellām poṟpaṭṭut tukil pōlavum āṅkāṅkuc collutaṟ kariya pēroḷiyoṭu tikaḻavum ulakamaṅkai nakaittāṟpōlap putumaiyuṟṟut tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkāla aḻakellām pāṭṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum. ā! iṅṅaṉan tōṉṟum avviṭiyaṟkāla aḻakiṉaik kaṇṭu viyanta vaṇṇamāy mīṉvalaiyoṭu kaṭaṟkaraiyil niṟkuñ cempaṭavaṉaik kāṭṭiṉuñ ciṟanta pulavaṉ yār?
av viṭiyaṟkālaiyilē mullai nilattu mēypparkaḷ āṉkaṉṟu kaḷait toḻuvattilē tāmpiṉāṟ kaṭṭivaittu āṉniraikaḷai aṭuttuḷḷa malaiccāraliṟ koṇṭupōyp paciya pul mēyaviṭṭut, tām maraniḻaliṟ cāyntiruntu koṇṭu, tamakketirē paccilaip pōrvai mēṟkoṇṭu kariya mukilkaḷ neṟṟi taḻuvik kiṭappap peruntaṉmaiyoṭu vāṉ aḷāvit tōṉṟum malaiyiṉai aṇṇāntu pārttavarāy avarkaḷ accamum makiḻcciyum aṭaiyumpōtu aṅkum pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum.
kātaliṉāṟ kaṭṭuṇṭa iḷaiñarum makaḷirum nekiḻāta kātalaṉpiṉ mikutiyāl tōḷoṭu tōḷpiṇaiyat taḻuvikkoṇṭu, malaiyaṭi vārattil uḷḷa pūñcōlaikaḷiṟ kaḷippāy ulavuntōṟun, tūṅkaṇaṅ kuruvikaḷ marakkiḷaikaḷil viyappāṉa kūṭu kaṭṭutalaiyum; marap pontukaḷiliruntu maṇippuṟākkaḷ kūvutalaiyum; āṇmayilkaḷ tam aḻakiya tōkaiyiṉai virittup peṭaimayil kaṇṭukaḷippa orupuṟam āṭutalaiyum; malaiyilirun toḻukum aruvinīr kūḻāṅkaṟ paṭaiyiṉ mēṟ ciluciluveṉṟu ōṭivantu ac cōlaiyiṉ oru pakkattuḷḷa āḻnta kuṭṭattil nirampit tuḷumpa, ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa centāmarai mukiḻkaḷ akaṉṟa ilaikaḷiṉ mēl itaḻkaḷai virittu mikac civappāy alartalaiyum virumpik kaṇṭu, naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻum pūkkaḷai maraṅkaḷiṉiṉṟun tāvip paṟittuk kariya kūntalil māṟi māṟi aṇintuñ, civakkap paḻutta kovvaikkaṉi pōṉṟa tam itaḻkaḷ aḻunta muttam vaittuk koṇṭun, tēṉ oḻukiṉāleṉa iṉiya nēyamoḻikaḷ pēcik koṇṭum avarkaḷ cellumiṭattu aṅkum pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum.
curuṅkac colluṅkāl eṅkellām namatuṇarvaik kavarkiṉṟa pēraḻaku ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭumō aṅkellām pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. āyiṉum, oru nallicaip pulavaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa pāṭṭuppōla atu nūliṉiṭattē kāṇap paṭuvatillaiyēyeṉiṉ; naṉku viṉāyiṉāy, oru nūliṉ kaṇ eḻutappaṭṭu, ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉ aḻakai namatuḷḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟak kāṭṭi namakku uvappuṇarvu payakkuñ colliṉ tokutiyāṉa pāṭṭu nūliṉkaṇ eḻutappaṭukiṉṟa vaṭivuṭaiya parupporuḷākum; ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉ aḻakōṭu oruṅkottu niṉṟu, kaṇ mutalāṉa pulaṉvaḻip pukuntu namakku uvappuṇarvu mikutiyiṉai varuvikkum pāṭṭu, vaṭivam illāta nuṇporuḷākum. iṅṅaṉamākaliṉ ulaka iyaṟkaiyilellām pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉpatu tuṇipēyā meṉka.
allāmalum, uyir vāḻnāḷil ovvoru nāḷum nammuṭaiya niṉaivukaḷellām uṇavu tēṭutalilum, poruḷ tokuppatilum, maṉaivimakkaṭku vēṇṭuvaṉa tiraṭṭik koṭuttalilum, piṟar iṭṭa ūḻiyañ ceytalilumākap palavāṟu citaṟi arumai perumaiyiṉṟik koṉṉē kaḻintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu kaḻintu pōkum makkaḷuṭaiya niṉaivu kaḷuñ coṟkaḷuñ ceyalkaḷum namakku iṉpantarāvākaliṉ avaṟṟai aṟiya vēṇṭumeṉa virumpuvārum ulakil yārum ilar. iṉi, ivvāṟu kaḻiyum nāṭkaḷil orōvorukāl avar aṟivu vaṟiya niṉaivukaḷiṉ vēṟākap pirintu, ulaka iyaṟkaiyaḻakiṟ paṭintu ataṉ vaṇṇamāyt tirintu teḷivuṟṟu viḷaṅkumpōtu, avvaṟiviṟ curantu perukum ariya periya karuttukkaḷaiyē nām aṟitaṟku mikaviḻaikiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉan tōṉṟum ariya periya karuttukkaḷiṉ kōvai oḻuṅkiṉaiyē pāṭṭeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṉṉum, makkaḷ vāḻnāḷ eṉkiṉṟa nīrōṭaiyilē varuniṉaivu kaḷāṉa kalaṅkaṟ perunīr perukic cellumpōtu, ulaka iyaṟkaiyeṉṉum malaik kukaikaḷilē arittu eṭuttuvanta aruṅkaruttukkaḷāṉa poṟṟukaḷ iṭaiyiṭaiyē āḻntu avvōṭaiyiṉ aṭinilattiṟ citarntu miṉṉikkiṭappa, nallicaippulavaṉ eṉṉum arippukkāraṉ mika viḻaintu muyaṉṟu appoṟcitarkaḷaiyellām oṉṟākap poṟukki eṭuttut taṉ matinuṭpa neruppiliṭṭu urukkip pacumpoṟ piṇṭamākat tiraṭṭit taruvatē pāṭṭu eṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṉṉum, makkaḷ aṟivu eṉkiṉṟa tittippāṉa ariya amiḻtam palavakaiyāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷoṭuṅ kalappuṟṟut tūyataṉṟāyp pōka, nallicaippulavaṉ taṉ pēraṟiviṉāl ataṉait teḷiya vaṭittu ataṉ iṉcuvaiyiṉai mikutip paṭutti, nāmellām ataṉaip parukip periyatōr āṟutalaṭaiyak koṭuppaṉ; aṅṅaṉaṅ koṭukkappaṭun tūya iṉiya aṟiviṉ viḷakkamum pāṭṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum. ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē miliṭṭaṉ eṉṉum āṅkilamoḻi valla nallicaip pulavarum, pāṭṭeṉpatu makkaḷ maṉa aṟiviṉiṉṟum vaṭittu iṟakkappaṭṭa tūya amiḻtam ām1 eṉṟu urai kūṟiṉār. itu niṟka.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam iyaṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa pāṭṭu ulaka iyaṟkaiyaḻakuṭaṉ peritum porunti naṭattal vēṇṭum. iṉṉum itaṉai nuṇuki nōkkumiṭattup pāṭṭup pāṭutalil vallavaṉāṉa nallicaip pulavaṉukkum ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉaip palavakai vaṇṇaṅkaḷāṟ kuḻaittu varaintu kāṭṭukiṉṟa ōviyakkāraṉukkum oṟṟumai mika uṇṭeṉpatu teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭum. āyiṉum, ōviyakkāraṉ varaikiṉṟa ōviyaṅ kaṭpulaṉukku maṭṭumē tōṉṟuvatākum; nallicaip pulavaṉ amaikkiṉṟa pāṭṭō kaṇ mutalāṉa pulaṉkaḷiṉ akattē viḷaṅkum uḷḷattilē ceṉṟu tōṉṟu vatākum. ōviyakkāraṉ tāṉ eḻuta eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruṭṭōṟṟattaip paṉmuṟaiyum nuṇṇitāka aḷantaḷantu pārttup piṉ ataṉait tiṟampaṭa varaintāl maṭṭum aṅṅaṉam varainta ōviyattaik kaṇṭu viyakkiṉṟōm; tāṉ virittu viḷakkamāy eḻutavēṇṭum pakutikaḷil avaṉ oru ciṟitu vaḻuvi viṭṭāṉāyiṉum avvōviyattiṉkaṇ namakku viyapput tōṉṟā toḻiyum. nallicaippulavaṉō aṅṅaṉam avaṉaippōl ovvoṉṟaṉaiyum virivāka viḷakkik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum varuttam uṭaiyāṉ allaṉ. ōviyakkāraṉ pulaṉ aṟivaip paṟṟi niṟpavaṉ; pulavaṉō maṉa aṟivaip paṟṟi niṟpavaṉ.
pulaṉaṟivō parupporuḷkaḷai virittaṟiyum iyalpi luḷḷatu; maṉavaṟivō appulaṉaṟiviṉ akattē niṉṟu nuṇṇitām poruḷaiyun tāṉē oru noṭiyil virittaṟiyum āṟṟal vāyntatu! ammamma! maṉavaṟiviṉ āṟṟalai yām eṉṉeṉṟu eṭutturaippēm! aṇuvai oru noṭiyil malaipōṟ perukac ceyyum, malaiyai maṟunoṭiyil or aṇuviṉuṅ kuṟukacceyyum. iṅṅaṉam viyappāṉa iyalpuṭaiya maṉavaṟiviṉai nallicaip pulavaṉ eṉṉum mantirakkāraṉ taṉ matinuṭpamākiya māttiraik kōlāl toṭṭa aḷavāṉē atu tiṭukkeṉṟeḻuntu avaṉ virumpiya vaṇṇamellāñ cuḻaṉṟu cuḻaṉṟāṭum.
iṉṉum itaṉaic ciṟitu viḷakkuvām. ōviyakkāraṉ accuṟuttum akaṉṟatoru kariya periya kāṭṭiṉai eḻutal vēṇṭumāyiṟ pala nāḷum palakālum ataṉ iyaṟkaiyiṉai aṟintaṟintu pārttup, pariya maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu ōṅki oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṇaintu veḷiccam pukāmal taṭai ceytu niṟṟalaiyum, akkāṭṭiṉ veḷittōṟṟa amaippiṉaiyum, maraṅkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiluḷḷa iṭukku veḷikaḷil namatu pārvai nuḻaiyuṅkāl avai tōṉṟun taṉmaiyiṉaiyum, uḷḷē iruḷ tiṇintu paraviyiruttalaiyum aṅkuḷḷavāṟē ciṟanta pala vaṇṇaṅkaḷaik kuḻaittu iraṭṭut tuṇiyiṉmēl mika varunti muyaṉṟu eḻutikkāṭṭal vēṇṭum; iḵtu avaṉukkup pala nāḷ viṉaiyāka muṭiyum. nallicaip pulavaṉō, pariya maraṅkaḷ aṭarntōṅkip piṇaintu niṟkum iruṇṭa kāṭu eṉṟu cila coṟkaḷait tiṟampaṭac cērttuk kūṟutal oṉṟiṉālēyē oru noṭippoḻutil avvōviyakkāraṉāluṅ kāṭṭa muṭiyāta oruperuviyap puṇarviṉai nam maṉattakattē viḷaivikkum āṟṟa  luṭaiyaṉāvaṉ. iḵtu ivaṉukku mika eḷitilē muṭivatoṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam maṉavuṇarviṉai eḻupputal mika eḷitilē ceyyak kūṭiyatoṉṟāyiṉum, ammaṉaviyalpiṉ nuṭpam uṇarntu avvāṟu ceyya vallarāṉa naṟperum pulavar ulakiṟ cilarēyāvar. pulavaṉuṭaiya tiṟamaiyellāñ cilvakaiyeḻuttiṟ palvakaip poruḷaik kāṭṭukiṉṟa arumperuñ ceykaiyiṉālētāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam pāṭṭuvaḻakkiṉ nuṭpa muṇarntu piṟa moḻikaḷiṟ pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya nallicaip pulavarkaḷ ōmar2, tāntē3, cekappiriyar4, miliṭṭaṉār5, kītē6, kāḷitācar mutaliyōrum, nañcen tamiḻil tiruvaḷḷuvar, nakkīraṉār, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār, māṅkuṭimarutaṉār, kapilar, cēkkiḻār mutalāṉōrum paṇṭaikkālattu ēṉai nallicaip pulavarumēyāvar. iṉṉum itaṉai virippiṟ perukumeṉṟañci ittuṇaiyiṉ niṟuttukiṉṟōm.



aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Areopagitica
2.	Homer 
3.	Dante
4.	Shakespeare
5.	Milton
6.	Goethe

2. paḻantamiḻp pāṭṭiṉ ciṟappiyalpu
iṉi, paṇṭaik kālattuc centamiḻp pulavarellārum ulaka iyaṟkaittiṟam piṟaḻāmal, ataṉai nuṇuki ārāyntu pāṭṭuppāṭum maṉavuṟuti mikutiyumuṭaiyarāyiruntaṉar. ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum oḷiviḷakkattaiyum eḻilaiyum mika viyantaṉar. tam maṉaṉuṇarviṟku icainta vaṇṇamellām ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉait tirittuk kūṟāmal, avvulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉ aḻakiṉ vaḻiyē tamataṟiviṉaip porunta vaittut tam niṉaiviṉai virivuceytu viḷakki makiḻntaṉar. im muṟaimai naṟperum pulavarkku iṉṟiyamaiyāc ciṟappiṉatām eṉṉuṅ karuttup paṟṟiyē iracikar1 eṉṉum āṅkila moḻi uraivalla āciriyar, kāṭṭu eṉṟa pulavaraip paṟṟic collavanta viṭattu avar tamatuṇarviṉ vaḻiyē ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉai niṟuttik koḷḷāmal, avvulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉ vaḻiyē tamatuṇar viṉai niṟutti niṉṟār eṉṟu pukaḻnteṭuttuk kūṟiṉār. ākavē, ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉ vaḻi niṉṟu pāṭṭup pāṭutalē arumaiyāmeṉpatum, atuvē nallicaip pulavarkku aṭai yāḷamām eṉpatum itaṉāl naṉku peṟappaṭum. paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷellārum in nuṇukkam iṉitaṟintu viḷaṅkiṉār kaḷeṉpataṟkup paḻaiya tamiḻp pāṭṭukkaḷē cāṉṟākum. eṉiṉum, itaṉai oru paḻaiya ceyyuḷ mukattāṉum oru ciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām.
 muḷitayir picainta kāntaḷ melviral
kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkaṅ kaḻāa tuṭīik
kuvaḷai yuṇkaṇ kuyppukai kamaḻat
tāṉtuḻan taṭṭa tīmpuḷip pākar
iṉiteṉak kaṇavaṉ uṇṭaliṉ
nuṇṇitiṉ makiḻntaṉṟu oṇṇutal mukaṉē.
eṉpatu kuṟuntokai2 eṉṉum paḻaiya tamiḻnūlil uḷḷa oru pāṭṭu. taṉ makaḷ taṉ kātaṟkaṇavaṉ vīṭṭil eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpataik kaṇṭaṟiyum poruṭṭuc ceṉṟa cevilittāy, avviruvarum mikka nēyamuṭaiyarāy vāḻvatu kaṇṭu, taṉṉuḷḷē makiḻntu colliyatāka itu pāṭappaṭṭiruk
kiṉṟatu. eṉ makaḷ vaṟṟakkāycciṉa kaṭṭit tayiraip picainta kāntaḷ malarmukiḻpōṟ civanta melliya viralkaḷāl, naṉku kaḻuvi veṇmaiyāṉa uyarnta āṭai camaiyal ceyyum viraiviṉāl iṭuppiṉiṉṟum aviḻntu kaḻala ataṉaik kaikaḻuvāmalē uṭuttukkoṇṭu, kuvaḷaip pūppōṉṟa mai tīṭṭiya taṉ kaṇkaḷilē tāḷippuc ceyyum pukai paṭṭu maṇakkavum ataṉaiyum pārātu, tāṉ tuṭuppiṉāl tuḻāvi mikka aṉpoṭu camaitta cuvai mikunta puḷippākiṉait taṉ kaṇavaṉ mikavum iṉitāyirukkiṉṟa teṉṟu collik koṇṭē uṇṇutalaip pārttu oḷimikunta neṟṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉ makaḷiṉ mukam uḷḷukkuḷḷē nuṭpamāy makiḻcci aṭaintatu eṉpatu tāṉ ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷ. pāruṅkaḷ! icceyyuḷiṉ iyaṟkaiyaḻakum, itaṉkaṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum maṉavuṇarviṉ iyalpum evvaḷavu arumaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa! kātaṟkaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyuṅ keḻumi iruntu illaṟam nikaḻttum oḻukkam mullai eṉappaṭumākaliṉ, ivvoḻukkam naṭaipeṟukiṉṟa mullai nilattiṟku ēṟpa muḷitayir picainta eṉṟār; eṉṉai? tayir pāl mutaliyaṉa āṉiraimikka mullai nilattiṟkē uriyavākaliṉ. taṉ kaṇavaṉ mēluḷḷa kātal mikutiyiṉāl ēvalarum piṟaruñ camaiyal ceyvataṟku oruppaṭātu tāṉē taṉ melliya civanta viralkaḷāl tayiraip picaitalum, kaṇavaṉ pacittiruppāṉē eṉṉum niṉaivāl viraintu camaiyal ceyyumpōtu iṭuppiṟkaṭṭiya uyarnta āṭai kaḻalavum picainta kaiyiṉaik kaḻuviviṭṭu uṭuppataṟkuṭ kālam nīṇṭu appuḷippākiṉ pataṅkeṭumeṉa uṇarntu akkaiyuṭaṉēyē avvuyarnta āṭaiyiṉaik kaṭṭikkoḷḷutalum, aṅṅaṉam picaintu tiruttiya puḷippākiṉait tāḷikkumpōtu mēl eḻum pukai taṉ kuvaḷaippūvaṉṉa kaṇṇiṟpaṭavum appuṟan tirumpiṉāl atu pataṅkeṭumē eṉṟu mukantirumpāmal ataṉai viraintu tuḻāvalum, aṅṅaṉa
mellān taṉ varuttattiṉaiyum pārātu camaitta cuvaimikka appuḷippākiṉaik kaṇavaṉ makiḻntuṇṇal kaṇṭu taṉ makiḻcci veḷiyē teriyāmal avaḷ mukam malarntu kāṭṭutalum, iyaṟkaiyē taṉakkuḷḷa nāṇattāl avaḷ mukañ ciṟitu kaviḻntu niṟka avaḷatu oḷimikka neṟṟiyē am makiḻccik kuṟippup pulaṉāka muṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutaluñ cila collil mika viḷaṅkakkūṟiya nuṇmai peritum viyakkaṟpālatoṉṟām. uḷḷamuvakkum mullai nilattiṟ kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum nēyamāy maruvivāḻum iyaṟkai ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ōviyam eḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōl evvaḷavu uṇmaiyākavum iṉitākavuñ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu! ipporuḷarumaiyōṭu ic ceyyuḷil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ ellām nīrmaṭaiyil teḷinīr moḻumoḻuveṉṟu ōṭuvatupōl ōcaiyiṉpam uṭaiyavāy oḻukutalum, oru collāyiṉum poruḷiṉṟi vēṇṭā kūṟalākāmal muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa poruṭṭoṭarpukku ēṟpa iṭaiyē muḻumuḻuc coṟkaḷāy amaintu niṟṟalum mikavum pārāṭṭaṟ pālaṉavākum eṉpatu.
iṉṉum paṇṭaik kālattut taṇṭamiḻp pulavar ulaka iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷai āṅkāṅkut tirintu kaṇṭu peruṅ kaḷippum perukiya maṉaveḻucciyum uṭaiyarāy, varuttamiṉṟi iṉitākap pāṭṭukaḷ pāṭiṉār eṉpatu avar tām virittuc collum poruḷkaḷukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭum uvamaikaḷāl naṉku pulaṉām. ōriṭattil māṉ kompaip paṟṟic collavantuḻi, irumpai muṟukkiṉāṟ pōluṅ kariya periya kompu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa irumpu tirittaṉṉamāyiru maruppu (akanāṉūṟu, 4) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil iruvar nēya oṟṟumaiyiṉaic colla vantapōtu kattiyuṟai ceyyuñ ciṟiya toḻilāḷaṉ arakkoṭu cērtta kallaip pōlap piriyōm eṉṉum poruḷpaṭac ciṟukārōṭaṉ3 payiṉoṭu cērttiya kaṟpōṟ piriyalam (akanāṉūṟu,1) eṉṟum, ōriṭattiṟ puṟanturuttiya naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷukku vēppampū mukaiyiṉai uvamaiyāka eṭuttu vēppunaṉaiyaṉṉa neṭuṅkaṭkaḷvaṉ (aiṅkuṟunūṟu, 30) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil vayal nel putitu īṉṟa paciya katirukkuc celvarkaḷ tamatu kutiraiyiṉ ucciyil tūkkik kaṭṭiya taiyal mūṭṭuḷḷa kavarimayirai uvamaiyāka eṭuttu muracuṭaiccelvar puraviccūṭṭum, mūṭṭuṟu kavari tūkkiyaṉṉa, ceḻuñcey nelliṉ cēyarip puṉiṟṟuk katir (akanāṉūṟu, 156) eṉṟum ōriṭattil maḻaiyillāta vāṉam pūttatu pōla ilai neruṅkiya mucuṇṭaic ceṭikaḷ veḷḷiya malarkaḷaip pūkka eṉṉum poruḷ pōtara maḻaiyil vāṉam mīṉ aṇintaṉṉa, kuḻaiyamal mucuṇṭai vāliya malara (akanāṉūṟu, 264) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil pañciṉ ṟoṭar nuṉipōlun talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya putarkaḷiṉ mēl ēṟippaṭarum iṇṭaik koṭikaḷiṉ nīril naṉainta taḷirkaḷ neyyil tōyttaṉapōla viḷaṅki iraṇṭāka iruḷaik kūṟupaṭuttiṉāṟpōl ovvoru taḷirum iraṇṭu kūṟupaṭṭaṉavāyk kariyaniṟattuṭaṉ acaiya eṉṉum poruḷpaṭat tuyttalaippūviṉ putalivar īṅkai, neytōyttaṉṉa nīrnaṉai antaḷir, iruvakiri iruḷiṉ īriya tuyalvara (akanāṉūṟu, 294) eṉṟum, ōriṭattiṟ paccai mañcaḷiṉ paciya mutukaip pōl cuṟṟilum poruttuṭampu uṭaiyaṉavāyk kaḻiyiṟ kiṭakkum iṟā mīṉ eṉpatu viḷaṅka muṟṟā mañcaṭ pacumpuṟaṅ kaṭuppac, cuṟṟiya piṇara cūḻkaḻi yiṟavu (naṟṟiṇai, 101) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil mayiliṉ aṭipōl mūṉṟu piḷavāy irukkum ilai kaḷaiyuṭaiya periya katiruḷḷa nocci eṉpatu tōṉṟa mayilaṭi ilaiyamākkural nocci eṉṟum, ōriṭattiṟ katir arintuviṭṭa tiṉaippayiriṉ tāḷpōṉṟa ciṟiya pacuṅkālai yuṭaiyavāy ōṭumnīril āralmīṉaip pārttuk koṇṭirukkum nārai eṉpatu pulappaṭat tiṉaittāḷ aṉṉa ciṟupacuṅ kāla, oḻukunīr āral pārkkuṅ kuruku (kuṟuntokai, 25) eṉṟum pōnta taṉmai naviṟciyaṇic coṟṟoṭarkaḷāṉē paḻaiya tamiḻp pulavariṉ viḻumiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷ aṟiviṉai iṉitu aṟintu koḷḷalām. iṉṉum ivaipōṉṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ nūṟunūṟākap perukkalāmēṉum, iṅku ataṟku iṭam
peṟutal kūṭāmaiyiṉ itaṉai ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟōm.
ittuṇai nuṭpamāṉa ulakiyaṟ poruḷ aṟivu paṇṭaik
kālattut taṇṭamiḻp pulavariṭaṅ kāṇappaṭutal pōla, maṟṟaimoḻikaḷil vallarāy viḷaṅkiya ēṉaip paḻampulavariṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭutal aritu. iṉṉum ivvāṟē paḻaiya tamiḻppulavar ulaka iyaṟporuṭkāṭcikaḷaip puṉainturaitta muṟaiyum, avvulaka iyaṟkaikkum makkaḷiyaṟkaikkum uḷḷa poruttam paṟṟi avar veḷiyiṭṭa ariya karuttukkaḷiṉ viḻuppamum pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭut tokai, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷiṟ parakkak kāṇalām. āṇṭuk kaṇṭu koḷka.
ti.mu. nāṉūṟu āṇṭu mutal ti.pi. nūṟāṇṭu varaiyil toṭarpuṟṟu viḷaṅkiya centamiḻ ilakkiya kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷiṉ iyaṟkaiyum, annūlkaḷukkum mullaippāṭṭiṟkum uḷḷa iyaipum, akkāla varalāṟum.
iṉi, tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟappataṟku muṟceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷilē mikavum pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya pulavar kālamum, avar piṟanta piṉnūṟṟāṇṭilē avvāṟu viḷaṅkiya pulavar kālamuñ centamiḻmoḻi mika uyarnta nilaiyilē iruntu tikaḻnta kālamākumeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟappataṟkumuṉ oru nāṉūṟu āṇṭum, avar piṟantapiṉ oru nūṟu āṇṭum cērntu muṭinta ōr ainnūṟāṇṭun tamiḻmoḻi maṟuvaṟṟa matipōṟ kalainirampi viḷaṅkiya kālamākum. ikkālattilē ciṟanta pulavar palartōṉṟip palavakaiyāṉa ariya periya centamiḻ nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉār. ip pulavarkaḷaip pōṟṟit tamiḻai vaḷampaṭuttaṟku āvalmikka aracar palarum vaḷḷal palarum āṅkāṅku mikkiruntaṉar. tamiḻ aracarkaḷ palar kalvi vaḷattāluñ celvavaḷattālum mēmpaṭṭum, pōr vallamaiyilum perumaiyaṭaintu, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉaip palaviṭaṅkaḷilum perukac ceyvatiṟ karuttūṉṟiṉarāy iruntār.
ikkālattilētāṉ, taṉakku oppum uyarvum iṉṟiviḷaṅ kāniṟkun tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum arumperunūl eḻutappaṭṭatu; cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutalāṉa ciṟanta tamiḻk kāppiyaṅkaḷum, paḻamoḻi, nāṉmaṇikkaṭikai mutalāṉa aṟanūlkaḷun tōṟṟamuṟṟu eḻuntaṉa ivvainnūṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉumpiṉṉumirunta tamiḻppulavarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭuc citaṟikkiṭanta aruntamiḻp pāṭṭukkaḷellām oruṅku tokukkap paṭṭu, akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, kalittokai mutaliya vakaivakait tokai nūlkaḷāka ikkālattilētāṉ oḻuṅku paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭaik kālattilē ceyyappaṭṭa tolkāppiyam eṉṉum ariyaperiya  tamiḻilakkaṇattil mikac ciṟanta pakutiyāṉa akapporuḷiṉ virivaiyellāñ curukki ataṉai vaṭitta piḻivāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ eṉṉum maṉaviyaṟkainūl paṉṉeṭuṅ kālamāka maṟaintukiṭantu piṉṉar ikkālattilētāṉ veḷivantu lāvalāyiṟṟu.
iṉṉum ikkālattilē iṉṟiyamaiyātu aṟiyaṟpālatāñ ciṟappiyalpu oṉṟuṇṭu. itaṟku muṉṉellān tamiḻ perumpāluñ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilēyē perukivantatu; maṟṟu ikkālattilō ataṉōṭu urai vaḻakkum viravip paravat toṭaṅkiṟṟu; col viḻuppamum poruḷ viḻuppamum potinta mika iṉiyatōr urai mika nuṇukkamāṉa aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiya nakkīrar eṉṉum nallicaip pulavarāl iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ eṉṉum nūlukku varaintu tarappaṭṭatu. ivvurai cūttirapporuḷai iṉitu viḷakkum poruṭṭē eḻutappaṭṭatāyiṉum, maṟṟai uraikaḷpōṟ curuṅkātu mika virintu iṉṟiyamaiyātu uṇaraṟpālaṉavām. arum peruntamiḻ nuṭpaṅkaḷellām oruṅku nirampip polikiṉṟatu. ākavē, ikkālattil mikac ciṟanta uraiyāciriyarāyt tōṉṟit, tamiḻ moḻiyiṟ pala vakaiyāṉa nalla cīrtiruttaṅkaḷellāñ ceytu, tamiḻp payiṟciyaip perukac ceytuvanta naṟperum pulavar āciriyar nakkīraṉār tāmeṉṟu aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. itaṟkup piṟkālattē vaṭamoḻik kalappāṟputitu tōṉṟiya viruttappā eṉpatu, ivvainnūṟāṇṭukaḷum virintu peruki vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷil eṭṭuṇaiyuṅ kāṇappaṭāmai peritum niṉaivukūraṟpāṟṟu.
pauttacamayat tōṟṟamum perukkamum
iṉi, iṅṅaṉan tamiḻ perukkamuṟṟu viḷaṅkutaṟku oru perun tuṇaikkāraṇamāy iruntatu yātu? eṉṟu ārāyap pukuvārkkup pautta camayam āṅkāṅku viḷakka muṟṟup paravi vantamaiyēyā meṉpatu pulappaṭum.
paṇṭaik kālattē āciyāk kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭaticaip pakkaṅkaḷil irunta āriyar, kuḷirnaṉi mikunta avviṭaṅ kaḷai viṭṭu, intiya nāṭṭiṟ kuṭipukutaṟkumuṉ, iv vintiyanāṭu muḻutum paraviyirunta makkaḷ tamiḻarē yāvar.4 tamiḻar irunta ivvintiya nāṭu perumpālum veppam mikunta nāṭātalāl itilirunta avarellāṅ kuḷir mikunta āciyāk kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakkēyuḷḷa āriya makkaḷaip pōl attuṇai uṭal valimai yuṭaiyarāka iruntilar. uṭampil uraṅkuṉṟiyirun tamaiyāl tamiḻar tamakkuḷḷē kalām viḷaittu oṟṟumai kulaitaṟku iṭampeṟutaliṉṟip perumpālum orumaippāṭu uṭaiyarāy nāṭ kaḻittaṉar. uṭalvaliviṉ kuṟaivāl avar maṉa aṭakkam peṟṟu etaṉaiyum āḻntaṟiyum iyalpuṭaiya rāyiruntaṉar. ulaka iyaṟkai yiluḷḷa aḻakiṉaik kaṇṭu viyantu avvaḷavil amaintu viṭāmal, avviyaṟkaiyiṉ uḷḷē nuḻaintu aṅkellām, piṟaḻāta ōr orumaippāṭum ataṉai aṅkē nilaipeṟutti maṟaintu kiṭakkum ōr uyirp poruḷiṉiruppuṅ kaṇṭaṟintu kaḷippaṭaintaṉar. aṅṅaṉam ivvulaka iyaṟkaiyil maṟaintu ūṭuruvik kiṭakkum avvuyirpporuḷiṉaiyē kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu tuṇintu, ataṉai maṉattāl niṉaintu vāyāl vāḻtti meyyāl vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭṭu vāḻntaṉar. avar tamatu uṭalvaliviṉ kuṟai pāṭṭāṟ palappala vakaiyāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ iyaṟṟi vaḻipaṭutaṟku oruppaṭārāyt taṉiyē ōr iṭattil maṉa amaitiyōṭu iruntu akkaṭavuṭporuḷai maṉattāṟ palakāl uṟaittu niṉaintu, ataṉāl aṟivāḻa muṭaiyarāyt tulaṅku vārāyiṉar.
ivar ivvāṟu iruppa, āciyāviṉ vaṭapakutikaḷil irunta āriyarō kuḷirāl uṭampu iṟuki mikka valivuñ curucuruppum uṭaiyarāyiruntaṉar; uṭampu valivu mikutiyum uṭaimaiyāluṅ, kuḷirum paṉiyum mikunta avvaṭapakutikaḷil uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ vēṇṭum aḷavu kiṭaittilāmaiyālum avarkaḷ oriṭattil amaitiyāy irukkappeṟāmal, tokuti tokutiyākap pala ticaikaḷiṟ pirintupōy aṅkaṅkuḷḷā roṭu pōr purintum allātavarkkuk kīḻaṭaṅkiyum āṅkāṅkuk kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntuvaralāyiṉar. avar maṟṟaiyōruṭaṉ pōr iyaṟṟappōṉa kālaṅkaḷilellān tāmē veṟṟi peṟum poruṭṭu ataṉaip peṟuvikkum uyirttuṇaiyai nāṭat talaippaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, tam muṉṉōril iṟantupaṭṭa varkaḷāṉa intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mittiraṉ mutaliyavariṉ āvikaḷaiyē teyvaṅkaḷeṉat tuṇintu vaḻipaṭalāṉār; vaḻipaṭum kālaṅkaḷilellān tām uṇavāka ayiṉṟuvanta palavakaiyāṉa vilaṅkukaḷaik koṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉ iṟaiccikaḷait tēvarkku ūṇeṉa ūṭṭi vēḷviceytum, vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷaip palavēṟu vakaiyavāyp perukki iyaṟṟiyum vantaṉar.
(irukku vētattiṉ mutaṉ maṇṭilattil uḷḷa, 33)
	intiraṉē, ellāmvallavaṉē, mikuntaporuṭ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷai oruṅku cērttukkoṇṭu emmuṭaṉ vāṇikañ ceyyātē!	(3)
	celvattiṉ mikka tayuvai nī taṉiyākavē niṉ kulicap paṭaiyāṟ koṉṟu, intiraṉē, nī niṉ tuṇaivaruṭaṉ ēkukiṉṟāy!
	toṉṟutoṭṭē caṭaṅkukaḷ ceyyārāṉa avarkaḷ, vāṉveḷikkuc cēyarāyp, palamukamāyt tappiyōṭi aḻintaṉar.	(4)
(51)
	āriyaraiyun tayukkaḷaiyum naṉṟāy vēṟupirittaṟintu koḷka! caṭaṅkukaḷ iyaṟṟāta avarkaḷait taruppaippuṟ parappuvōṉpāl oppuvittiṭuka!	(8)
(53)
	irijivāṉ kīḻppaṭiyāṉāy avarkaḷai muṟṟukai ceyta annāḷil, vaṅkiritaṉuṭaiya nūṟu kōṭṭai kaḷaiyum nī aḻittaṉaiyaṉṟō!	(8)
	tuṇaivarillāta cucuravarkaḷuṭaṉ pōrpuriyum poruṭṭu, aṟupatiṉā yirattut toṇṇuṟṟoṉpatu kālāṭkaḷuṭaṉ paṭaiyeṭuttu vanta makkaḷuḷ aracarāṉa irupatiṉmaraiyum, ō intiraṉē, paranta pukaḻuṭaiyāy, nī ellāvaṟṟaiyum mēṟkaṭanta tēr uruḷaikaḷāl aḻittuḷaiyaṉṟō!					(9)

	intiraṉē, tayuvaitterintu avaṉmēl niṉkaṇaiyai ēvuka! āriyaṉuṭaiya āṟṟalaiyuñ ciṟappaiyum mikutip paṭuttuka!	(3)
(163)
	teyvattai nōkki niṉaitta maṉattiṉatāy valiya kutirai veṭṭappaṭutaṟku muṉvantu niṟkiṉṟatu.
	ataṟku uṟaviṉatāka veḷḷāṭum ataṟkumuṉ ōṭṭap paṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟatu; iruṭiyarum pāṭakarum ataṉpiṉ  varukiṉṟaṉar.						(12)
	akkutirai mikacciṟanta koṭṭilukku vantirukkiṉṟatu, taṉ tāy taṉ tantaiyiṉpāl vantirukkiṉṟatu.
	naṉku varavēṟkappaṭṭu iṉṟu atu tēvarkaḷpāṟ cellum; ataṉaip paliyākak koṭuppavaṉukku atu pala naṉkoṭai yiṉait tarum	(13)
eṉṉum ivaipōṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷāl avar avviyalpu uṭaiyarātal tuṇiyappaṭum. ivviyalpuḷḷa āriyar intiyāviṉuṭ pukuntapōtu aṅkē tamakku muṉṉirunta tamiḻariṟ ciṟitu karuntōṟṟam uṭaiyarāy iruntavar tamakkellān tayukkaḷ, tācarkaḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvārāyiṉar. kirēkkar maṟṟai nāṭṭavaraip pārpēriyar eṉṟum, tamiḻar ēṉaiyōrai milēccar eṉṟum aḻaittal pōla āriyarun tamiḻariṟ cilarai avvāṟu peyariṭṭaḻaittaṉar. tamiḻar muṉṉāḷil āriyaraiyellām milēccareṉṟu aḻaittamai milēccar āriyar eṉṉun tivākara piṅkalantaic cūttirattāl naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. uṭal valimai mikavum uṭaiya āriyar intiyāviṉuṭ pukutalun tamiḻariṟ cilar avaroṭu pōr purintu tōlviyaṭaintaṉar; cilar amaitiyiṉ poruṭṭu malaikaḷiluṅ kāṭukaḷilum pōy iruntaṉar. cilar kaṭum pōr malaintu āriyarai veṉṟaṉar; cilar tāntām irunta iṭamviṭṭup peyarāmal āriyarai viruntāka ēṟṟu avaruṭaṉ uṟavāṭi avar vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟait tān taḻuviyun tam vaḻakka  oḻukkaṅkaḷai avar taḻuvumāṟu ceyvittum avarōṭu orumaiyuṟṟu vāḻat toṭaṅkiṉar. ivviruvakai iṉattārum oruvarōṭu oruvar maruvi vāḻum nāṭkaḷil avaravar tattamakkē uriya vaḻakkavoḻukkaṅkaḷai muḻuvatun tirittup piṟaḻtti viṭāmal, avaitammiṟ perumpāṉmaiya vaṟṟai muṉṉiruntapaṭiyē vaittukkoṇṭu kaṭaippiṭittu oḻukiṉar.
ikkālattil āriyaruṭ kurukkaḷmār palar tōṉṟip palavakaiyāṉa vēḷvikaḷ ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutti avaṟṟait tamiḻa aracar utaviyāṟ ceytu vantaṉar; appōtutāṉ av vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum muṟaikaḷai mika viritteḻutip pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. ellai illāta ēḻai vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷaik kolai ceytu iyaṟṟappaṭum vēḷvikaḷum vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷum āriyak kurukkaḷmārkaḷiṉ piṟaḻcci aṟivāl eṅkum mikunta varavē, uyirkkolaikku iyaṟkaiyilē uṭampaṭāta tamiḻaril aṟivāṉmikka cāṉṟōrkaḷ ‘iṅṅaṉan tītaṟṟa uyirkaḷiṉ uṭampaic citaittu vēḷvikaḷ ceytalāṟ pōtarum payaṉ eṉṉai? eṉṟu tam āriya naṇparuṭaṉ nayamāyk kalantu vaḻakkiṭṭu avariṟ cilarait tamvaḻippaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉat tamiḻariṉ aṟivāḻa ārāycciyiṉai āriya naṉmakkaḷ cilar tāmun taḻuvi oḻukap pukunta kālattilētāṉ upaniṭataṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivvupaniṭataṅkaḷ āriyarkku eṭṭātirunta aṟaṅkaḷai aṟivuṟutti, avar ceytupōnta uyirkkolaiyiṉai niṟuttutaṟ poruṭṭākat tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavāmeṉpataṟku av vupaniṭantaṅ kaḷilēyē maṟukkappaṭāta cāṉṟukaḷ pala virukkiṉṟaṉa5 iṅṅaṉam uṭaniṭataṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa piṉṉarum vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷaip paliyiṭṭuc ceyyum vēḷvikaḷ ciṟituṅ kuṟaipaṭāmal āriyarkkuḷ mikuntu vantamaiyāṉum, āriyak kurukkaḷmār taṅkoḷkaikku iṇaṅkāta  tamiḻaraiyum atu ceyyumpaṭi valintu varuttiṉamaiyāṉum āriyarkkun tamiḻarkkum itaṉporuṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷum etirvaḻakkukaḷum nēra, avvamayattil vaṭa nāṭṭilirunta tamiḻa aracakulattiṟ kautamacākkiyar eṉpār tōṉṟip paḻaiya tamiḻmakkaḷ ārāyntu vanta ariya nalloḻukka muṟaikaḷai eṭuttu virittuc collap pukuntār. kallāta makkaḷ maṉamuṅ karaintu urukumpaṭi mikka irakkattuṭaṉ nalloḻukkaṅkaḷiṉ viḻuppattai eṭuttu virittu, in nalloḻukkaṅkaḷai oruvaṉ vaḻuvāmaṟ ṟaḻuvi naṭappaṉāyiṉ avaṉukku ellāt tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭum niruvāṇam eṉṉun tūyanilai tāṉē varumeṉṟum, aṟivillāta ēḻai uyirkaḷai āyiramāyiramākak koṉṟu vēḷvi vēṭṭalāl mēlumēlun tīviṉaiyē viḷaiyumallatu nalviṉai eytāteṉṟuṅ kautamar aruḷkaṉintu aṟivuṟuppārāyiṉar. makkaḷ uḷḷattai eḷitilē kavarntu urukkumpaṭiyāṉa kautamar koḷkaikaḷ cila nāḷilē eṅkum paravalāyiṉa. makkaḷellārum āriyak kurukkaḷmār coṟkaḷil aiyuṟavu koṇṭu tam aṟivāl nallaṉa palavum ārāyappukuntaṉar. eṅkum  avaravar tattaṅkaruttukkaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟum nuṭpaṅkaḷait tārāḷamāy veḷiyiṭat tuṇintaṉar. pirāñcu tēyattiṟ6 ṟōṉṟiyatai yotta oru periya māṟutal eṅkum uṇṭāva tāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam oru periya māṟutal intiya nāṭu muḻuvatuñ cuḻaṉṟuvarumpōtu, teṉṉāṭṭiluḷḷa tamiḻarun tām tamatuḷḷattē ārāyntu vaitta ariya periya nuṇporuḷkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭut tamatu paṇṭait tamiḻmoḻiyiṉaip paṇṭaināḷiṟ pōlavē peritum vaḷampaṭuttum ariyamuyaṟciyil talai niṉṟār. iṅṅaṉan tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟappataṟkumuṉ oru nāṉūṟu āṇṭum piṉ oru nūṟāṇṭum mika virintu perukiya camaya viḷakkamē akkālattiṟ ṟamiḻmoḻiyiṉkaṇ arumperuñ centamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala tōṉṟutaṟku oru peruṅkāraṇamāyiṟṟu eṉṟu teḷivuṟa aṟital vēṇṭum. āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār iyaṟṟiya ariyaperiya tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kollāmai pulāluṇṇāmai, oḻukkamuṭaimai eṉṉun tamiḻark kuriya aṟivāḻa nuṭpap poruḷkaḷ palakālum palaviṭattum eṭuttu vaṟpuṟuttappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ip poruḷkaḷellām pautta camaya nūlkaḷi
lirun teṭuttuc collappaṭṭaṉa eṉpārun, tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār pauttarē eṉpārum uḷar. iyaṟkaiyilē tamiḻarkkuriya oḻukkaṅkaḷiṉ viḻuppattaiyē kautamar eṉṉun tamiḻpperiyār viḷakka vantamaiyāl avvoḻukka varicaikaḷ avar coṉṉapiṉ eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu poruntātu. tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār mutaliya cāṉṟōr, tamakkun tammiṉattārkkum iyaṟkaiyilē tōṉṟiya arumperuṅ karuttuk kaḷaiyē pautta camayam yāṇṭum virintu paranta kālattil taṭaiyiṉṟic collutaṟku iṭampeṟṟārākaliṉ, akkaruttukaḷ tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārkkuṅ kautamacākkiyarkkum potuvāvaṉavēyām eṉṟu tuṇika.
 avicorintu āyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
uyircekuttu uṇṇāmai naṉṟu	(kuṟaḷ. 259)
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷil āciriyar āriyamakkaḷ ceytu pōnta vēḷvi viṉaiyai maṟuttuk kollāmaiyiṉ ciṟappai valiyuṟuttik kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. iṉṉum ivvāṟē āciriyar āṅkāṅku āriyamakkaḷ ceytu pōnta maṟṟai viṉaiccaṭaṅkukaḷaiyum maṟuttuk kūṟutal kaṇṭu koḷka. īṇṭu avaiyellām eṭutturaippiṟ perukum.
iṉi, ivvāṟu oru kālattil naṭaipeṟum oḻukkaṅkaḷukkum ak kālattiṟ ṟōṉṟum nūlkaḷukkum periyatōr iyaipu uṇṭeṉpataṉai viḷakkutaṟ poruṭṭē ivvōr ainnūṟṟāṇṭiṉkaṇ naṭaipeṟṟa nikaḻcciyiṉai oru ciṟitu virittuk kūṟiṉēm. oru nūliṉ iyalpai uḷḷavāṟu uṇartaṟku annūl eḻutappaṭṭa kālattiṉ iyaṟkai iṉṟiyamaiyātu aṟiyaṟpālatākum. itupaṟṟiyē āṅkilamoḻiyil nuṭpavārāyccikaḷ pala eḻutiya uviliyam miṇṭō7 eṉṉum ārāycci uraikārar, kālappōkku eṉpatu iṉṉateṉṟu toṭṭu aṟiyappaṭāta ōr iyaṟkai vāyntatu; aḵtu akkālattu makkaḷ iyaṟṟum nūlkaḷiluṅ, kottu vēlaikaḷilum, uṭaikaḷilum, avarkaḷ naṭāttum vāṇika vāḻkkaiyilum, avarkaḷ amaikkun toḻiṟ kaḷaṅkaḷilum ellān taṉ aṭaiyāḷattaip patiya iṭukiṉṟatu. ***oru pulavaṉum oru kāla iyaṟkaiyiṉ vaḻiniṉṟē nūl eḻutupavaṉāvaṉ; akkāla iyaṟkai allatu am makkaḷ oppuravu avaṉ eḻutuvaṉavaṟṟai ellān taṉ uruvākki avaṟṟiṟkut taṉ niṟattai ūṭṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉai nam kaṇṭaṟivataṟku ak kālattiṉ potu iyaṟkaiyum, ataṉkaṇ avaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa makkaḷ naṭaiyiṉiyaṟkaiyum, avaṉ irukkum iṭattiṉ iyaṟkaiyum iṉṟiyamai yātu ārāyaṟpālaṉa vākum8 eṉṟu mika nuṭpamāka eṭuttu moḻintiṭṭār. atu kiṭakka.
iṉi, ivvōr ainnūṟāṇṭiṟ ṟōṉṟiya nūlkaḷellām perumpālum akkāla iyaṟkai taṅkaṇ etirtōṉṟi viḷaṅkap peṟum oru taṉmaiyuṭaiyavākumeṉṟu terital vēṇṭum. aṟivu āḻamiṉṟi āriya makkaḷ ceytupōnta vīṇāṉa veṟuñcaṭaṅkukaḷiṟ kaṭṭuppaṭāmal taṉiyē pirintuniṉṟa tamiḻ makkaḷ, tam paṇṭaiyāciriyarkaḷ ceṉṟa muṟaiyē ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉaiyum, makkaḷiyaṟkaiyiṉaiyum uḷḷuruvi nuḻaintu ārāyntu tāṅkaṇṭa ariya poruḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷai amaittu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉār. ākavē, ulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉaiyum, makkaḷ iyaṟkaiyiṉaiyum ārāyum ārāycci ikkālattut tōṉṟiya nūlkaṭkellām potutn taṉmaiyāku meṉṟuṇarntu koḷka.
iṉi, ivvulaka iyaṟkaiyiṉai ārāyum nūlkaḷ ellām puṟapporuḷn eṉavum, makkaḷiyaṟkaiyiṉai ārāyvaṉavellām akapporuḷ eṉavun tollāciriyarāl vakukkap paṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ akapporuḷ eṉpatu āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālāraiyuñ ceṟiyap poruttuvatāy, maṟṟellā uṇarvukaḷaiyum niṉaivukaḷaiyun taṉakkuk kīḻāka niṟuttit tāṉ avaṟṟiṉmēl amarntu taṉakku nikariṉṟip perumaiyuṭaṉ tōṉṟuvatāy, iṉpamun tuṉpamumellān tōṉṟu taṟkut tāṉ nilaikkaḷaṉāy, ellā ulakaṅkaḷum ellāp poruḷkaḷun taṉṉaic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu cellat tāṉ avaṟṟiṉ iṭaiyē ciṟitun tiripiṉṟi nilaipeṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatāy uḷḷa aṉpu allatu kātal9 eṉpataṉai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu makkaḷiyaṟkai muḻuvatūum oruṅkē ārāyvatākum. iṉip, puṟapporuḷ eṉpatu makkaḷ ulaka iyaṟkaiyuṭaṉ poruntit tamakku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavāṉa palvakai muyaṟcikaḷaiyum muṟṟup peṟuvittaṟ poruṭṭuc ceyyun toḻil vēṟupāṭukaḷum piṟavun teḷḷitiṉ ārāyvatām.
iṉip potuvāka ellā māntarkkum uriya iyaṟkai yiṉaip pakutturaippatākaliṉ, akapporuḷ oḻukkam payiṉṟuvarum pāṭṭukkaḷiṟ ciṟappāka or āṇmakaṉaiyum oru peṇ makaḷaiyum eṭuttu vaittu, aṉṉavar tamakkuriya peyarcolli avai tām eḻutap paṭutal illai. ellā makkaṭkum potuvāy varukiṉṟa aṉpiṉāl nikaḻum aintiṇai oḻukkattai oruvar iruvarkku varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟutal, avvaṉpiṉ aintiṇaiyoḻukkam ēṉaiyōrkku illaiyām pōlum eṉa malaivu tōṟṟuvittu vaḻuvāy muṭintiṭumākaliṉ, appāṭṭukkaḷ ellāṅ kuṟittu oruvar peyar collāmalē varaiyappaṭum eṉpatu teḷintu koḷka. itu kaṭaippiṭiyāy uṇarttutaṟkē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
 makkaḷ nutaliya akaṉain tiṇaiyuñ
cuṭṭi oruvar peyarkoḷap  peṟā ar?10
eṉṟu kiḷantu kūṟiṉār.
iṉi, itupōṟ potuvākavaṉṟi, makkaḷuḷ ovvoruvarun tattam muyaṟci vēṟupāṭukaḷukku ēṟpap palvakaippaṭṭa uṇarvum palvakaippaṭṭa ceyalum uṭaiyarāy ulakanaṭaiyaṟintu oḻukuvarākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉamāṉa avartam puṟapporuḷ oḻukkam payiṉṟuvarum pāṭṭukkaḷil avvavarkkē uriya peyar paṇpu ceyal mutaliyaṉa ellāṅ kiḷanteṭuttuk kūṟi maṟṟu avai eḻutappaṭum eṉka. oruvar paṇpuñ ceyalum ēṉaiyoruvar paṇpuñ ceyalum pōlaṉṟi ulakanaṭaiyiṟ perumpāṉmaiyum vēṟupaṭṭu veḷiyē tōṉṟik kiṭattalāl, aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟum paṇpu ceyalkaḷaik kūṟum puṟattiṇaip pāṭṭukkaḷil avvappaṇpu ceyalkaṭku uriyār peyar kūṟal vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatēyām eṉka. inneṟi aṟivuṟuttutaṟ poruṭṭē āciriyar tolkāppi yaṉār,
 puṟattiṇai maruṅkiṟ porunti ṉallatu
akattiṇai maruṅkiṉ aḷavuta lilavē11
 eṉṟu kūṟiṉār
iṉi, akam puṟam eṉṉum ivviruvakai oḻukkamuṅkalantu varum pāṭṭukkaḷil akavoḻukkamē perumpālum muṉṉum piṉṉun toṭarpuṟṟuccella, ataṉ iṭaiyē oru puṟavoḻukkañ ciṟukivaru māyiṉ, avaṟṟuḷḷum oruvar peyar kuṟittuc collappaṭuvatillai. avvāṟaṉṟi, avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum oru puṟavoḻukkamē toṭarpuṟṟuc cella iṭaiyē or akavoḻukkaṅ kuṟuki varumāyiṉ avaṟṟuḷ avvoḻukkam uṭaiyār peyar paṇpu mutalāyiṉa kiḷantu collappaṭum. ivvāṟaṉṟi akappuṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum iṇaintu oppa varumāyiṉ aṅkum ammakkaḷ peyar paṇpu mutalāyiṉa kiḷantu collappaṭum eṉpatu aṟika. iṅkuc collappaṭṭa ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ivvainnūṟāṇṭiṟ piṟanta nūlkaḷilellām iṉitu kāṇappaṭum.
